  "description": "Every succeeded registration and VM definition from the Core managed journal, plus the host's appliance deployments, queued work and terminal outcomes; at most 10000 entries per resource list and 30000 outcomes; each list is omitted when empty. Pool, host and resource IDs inside entries are host-owned and stable across Controllers: a host's self-registered default entry carries its pool ID when the host knows it (hosts enrolled with host-owned identity), otherwise its host ID as a placeholder until adoption. Adoption records each entry under the host's current pool and host, which is a no-op for hosts that presented their own identity at enrollment; that is how a replaced Controller recovers a pool from its master. Entries the Controller already holds under the same ID and kind are silently current. Any other entry it cannot adopt (invalid, conflicting ID/path/source/name, references not adopted on the host, or missing from libvirt) is recorded once per host and entry as a needs_attention operation (request_id adopt-attention:<host>:<kind>:<id>, actor system, error prefixed 'not adopted from host report:') with an audit entry adopt.attention naming the reason; the record is marked succeeded once the entry is adopted.       "description": "guest-get-osinfo pretty name; absent unless agent is connected. Maximum 4096 UTF-8 bytes."
    },
    "hostname": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "guest-get-host-name; absent unless agent is connected. Maximum 200 UTF-8 bytes."
    },
    "addresses": {
      "type": [
        "array",
        "null"
      ],
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/GuestAddress"
      },
      "maxItems": 32,
      "description": "guest-network-get-interfaces addresses without loopback or link-local entries; at most 32."
    },
    "clipboard": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "ready",
        "waiting",
        "no-channel"
      ],
      "description": "ready: the guest opened the qemu-vdagent channel (spice-vdagent is running) so the console clipboard works both ways; waiting: the channel exists but nothing in the guest opened it; no-channel: the VM was defined before console clipboard support and must be recreated to gain it."
    },
    "resize": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "description": "True only when the guest can actually follow a console resize request: the VM has a VirtIO display and the guest has opened the qemu-vdagent channel, which is the agent that applies the new resolution. A VirtIO display alone only means the host would send the request; a guest without the driver ignores it (BUG-0201)."
    },
    "media": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "loaded",
        "empty",
        "no-drive"
      ],
      "description": "The live CD drive of the running VM: loaded when its tray holds media, empty when the drive holds none (a guest can eject its own disc, for example an installer after the base system), no-drive when the VM has no CD drive. Absent from Core builds that do not observe it. It reports what the guest sees; the durable media_id changes only through a media request."
    },
    "drivers_media": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "loaded",
        "empty",
        "no-drive"
      ],
      "description": "The same live observation as media, for the VM's second (driver disc) CD drive. Absent from Core builds that define only one CD drive."
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "agent",
    "clipboard",
    "resize"
  ],
  "x-go-type": "protocol.GuestInfo",
  "description": "Observed inside a running VM by its host through host-local virtio channels: guest agent facts and console channel states. Observation only; a connected agent is not proof of application health."
}

UpdateVMRequest
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Omit, or the VM UUID from the path."
    },

    },

      "type": "string",

    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[^/\\u0000-\\u001f\\u007f-\\u009f\\ufffe\\uffff]+$",
      "description": "Nonblank VM name, at most 200 UTF-8 bytes (the byte limit is additionally enforced by Controller and Core). Spaces, case, dots, punctuation and Unicode are preserved exactly. Slashes, control characters and XML-invalid U+FFFE/U+FFFF are rejected. Core checks uniqueness on the host, journals the name and reports it for Controller adoption; failed or uncertain creations retain their reservation. Upgrade Controller and Core together to use the expanded naming syntax.",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 200
    },
    "description": {
      "type": "string",

    },

    },
    "media_id": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Install ISO (Media.id), empty for none. The ISO must be imported and verified on the placement host; it may live on any storage that host can read and does not need to share a repository with the VM's disks. Every VM has the CD drive, so a non-zero boot.media_boot_order does not require media_id: an emptied drive keeps its place in the boot order."
    },
    "start_after_create": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "const": false,
      "description": "Must be false; use the power controls to start a VM."
    },
    "autostart": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "description": "libvirt autostart, set while the stopped guest is defined so the host starts this VM on boot. It is not a start."
    },
    "request_id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "revision": {
      "type": "integer",
      "description": "Ignored; Controller assigns current + 1."
    }
  },

    "host_id",

    "guest_os",
    "cpu",
    "memory",
    "disks",
    "nics",
    "boot",
    "media_id",
    "start_after_create",
    "autostart",
    "request_id"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "description": "The complete desired definition of an existing VM plus a stable request_id. Disks are matched to the current definition by name (storage, size and provisioning of an existing disk cannot change); interfaces by MAC, so send existing interfaces with the address the VM list reports and leave mac empty only for new ones. Controller answers 400 when an interface at an existing index has an empty mac and the request removes an interface or that interface's network, model or MTU differs from the current interface at that index; an unchanged interface or one appended past the current count may still omit it. Changing an existing interface's MAC is treated as removing that interface and adding a new one, which a running VM accepts."
}
